skonester_crozier_cheats_setup = {
	save_scope_as = skonester_crozier_cheats_p if = { limit = { exists = var:skonester_crozier_cheats_target } var:skonester_crozier_cheats_target = { save_scope_as = skonester_crozier_cheats_t } } if = { limit = { exists = global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_other } global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_other = { save_scope_as = skonester_crozier_cheats_o } } if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_tier } } set_global_variable = { name = skonester_crozier_cheats_tier value = 2 } } if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_power } } set_global_variable = { name = skonester_crozier_cheats_power value = 1 } }
}

skonester_crozier_cheats_schedule = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = skonester_crozier_cheats_tick_pending } } if = { limit = { exists = global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_freq global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_freq = 1 } set_global_variable = { name = skonester_crozier_cheats_tick_pending value = yes days = 9 } scope:skonester_crozier_cheats_p = { trigger_event = { id = skonester_crozier_cheats.9000 days = 7 } } } else_if = { limit = { exists = global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_freq global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_freq = 3 } set_global_variable = { name = skonester_crozier_cheats_tick_pending value = yes days = 92 } scope:skonester_crozier_cheats_p = { trigger_event = { id = skonester_crozier_cheats.9000 days = 90 } } } else = { set_global_variable = { name = skonester_crozier_cheats_tick_pending value = yes days = 32 } scope:skonester_crozier_cheats_p = { trigger_event = { id = skonester_crozier_cheats.9000 days = 30 } } } }
}

	if = { limit = { global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_tier = 1 } scope:skonester_crozier_cheats_p = { add_prestige = 1000 } } else_if = { limit = { global_var:skonester_crozier_cheats_tier = 3 } scope:skonester_crozier_cheats_p = { add_prestige = 100000 } } else = { scope:skonester_crozier_cheats_p = { add_prestige = 10000 } }
